PRIMARY VIEW 1

Case Overview

CASE_RUNNER_V1_RUN · HSP90

HSP90_NTD_EXPOSED_PAPER_BLIND_V1

Given the available HSP90 NMR evidence and the supplied seeded MD trajectories, what aspects of the conformational relationship between the simulated paths and experimentally characterized ATP-lid states are supported descriptively, what remain unresolved, what stronger claims must be refused, and what additional evidence would discriminate the remaining interpretations?

Artifact integrityPASS
Current gatePENDING_DOMAIN_REVIEW
Recorded terminal stateNOT_CALCULATED_BY_CASE_RUNNER

Sources and locators

HSP90_NMR_METHODS_RESULTSPRIMARY_PAPER_METHODS_RESULTS

Henot et al., Nature Communications 2022, DOI 10.1038/s41467-022-35399-8; local text derivative Results lines 113-340 and Methods lines 488-623.

Declared facts

  • The source studies the HSP90alpha N-terminal domain and ATP-lid segment using solution NMR measurements.
  • The Methods describe wild-type, R46A, and R60A construct-specific NMR measurements, including relaxation-dispersion experiments.
  • The public packet supplies no validated numerical forward model from the NMR readouts to a local MD-derived observable.
  • The public packet does not establish that NMR and seeded MD sample the same physical conditions or estimand.

Excluded or unresolved context

  • Abstract
  • Discussion
  • Conclusion
  • Supplementary interpretation
HSP90_MD_DISTANCE_TRACEFROZEN_DERIVED_TRAJECTORY_OBSERVABLE

frozen_input_manifest_v1.json assets HSP90_CA46_CA60_DISTANCE_40X1021, HSP90_TRAJECTORY_IDS, and HSP90_TRAJECTORY_GROUP_MANIFEST.

Declared facts

  • The input is an ordered 40-trajectory by 1021-frame C-alpha distance observable for residue IDs 46 and 60.
  • Each row has one frozen trajectory identifier and one declared seed-lineage label; 20 rows retain R46A_ES and 20 retain R60A_GS labels.
  • Frames are correlated within a trajectory; the trajectory is the primary supplied statistical unit.
  • The packet does not assign states, estimate populations or kinetics, evaluate global equilibration, or supply a validated NMR forward model.

Excluded or unresolved context

  • Discussion
  • Conclusion
  • Prior trajectory result files
  • Precomputed route output

Authority boundary

This runner records exposed-development proposals and deterministic downstream behavior only. It does not compute a terminal verdict, source-science approval, broad HSP90 closure, ADK dynamics portability, or Agent effectiveness.

    "equilibrium population",
    "kinetic rate",
    "free energy",
    "mechanism",
    "numerical NMR-to-MD agreement"
  ]

{
  "admission": {
    "proposal": {
      "case_id": "HSP90_NTD_EXPOSED_PAPER_BLIND_V1",
      "proposed_case_facts": {
        "case": {
          "declared_request_scope": [
            "R46A and R60A construct-specific trajectory observables",
            "NMR-derived ATP-lid conformational descriptors",
            "Descriptive correspondence between MD distance traces and NMR measurements"
          ],
          "forbidden_upgrades": [
            "State assignment to ATP-lid conformations",
            "Population estimation for any conformational states",
            "Kinetic rate constant derivation",
            "Validated NMR-to-MD forward model",
            "Equilibration assessment for trajectory ensemble"
          ],
          "intended_use": "Descriptive characterization of the relationship between C-alpha distance observables from seeded MD trajectories and ATP-lid conformational descriptors reported in NMR experiments",
          "requested_claim_level": "DESCRIPTIVE_ONLY",
          "scientific_claim": "The available evidence supports only a descriptive correspondence between the C-alpha distance observable (residues 46-60) extracted from seeded MD trajectories and the ATP-lid conformational descriptors derived from solution NMR measurements. No validated forward model links the trajectory observables to NMR readouts. The physical conditions and estimand sampled by NMR experiments and MD simulations remain unestablished as congruent."
        },
        "edges": [
          {
            "bridge_status": "NO_VALIDATED_BRIDGE",
            "condition_relation": "NMR_MD_CONDITION_UNESTABLISHED",
            "edge_id": "EDGE_NMR_MD_DESCRIPTIVE_BRIDGE",
            "left_source_id": "HSP90_NMR_METHODS_RESULTS",
            "relation_type": "DESCRIPTIVE_CORRESPONDENCE",
            "right_source_id": "HSP90_MD_DISTANCE_TRACE",
            "shared_error_status": "UNKNOWN",
            "validation_independence": "UNVALIDATED"
          }
        ],

INTEGRITY_ERROR_EXAMPLEREQUIRED_ARTIFACT_MISSING

If a required case/run artifact is absent, malformed, cross-case, or stale, build_case_view raises CaseViewIntegrityError and the case is not rendered. This example is a visible fail-closed state, not an active error in HSP90 or ADK.

NDSR-F01R01PENDING_DOMAIN_REVIEW

Family: F01_CLAIM_CONTRACT_AND_CEILING
Runtime subrule: F01R01_CASE_CLAIM_DECLARATION

Locator: Wankowicz and Bonomi 2026, PDF p.5, intended-use evaluation passage (local parsed derivative lines 321-341) and Conclusion (lines 388-392)

Locator status: REVIEW_DERIVATIVE_LOCATOR_REQUIRES_PRIMARY_PASSAGE_CHECK

Passage kind: UPSTREAM_REVIEW_DERIVATIVE_NOT_PRIMARY_VERBATIM

Source packet status: LOCATOR_REVIEWED_FOR_PROPOSED_CONTROL_LOGIC

Traceability note: No separate traceability note recorded.

Short passage: Primary-source observation: whether conformer states and weights are adequate depends on intended use; experimental agreement, population shifts above uncertainty, and state-function association place different demands on an ensemble.

Atomic statement: The paper states that intended use changes what must be evaluated: experimental comparison emphasizes accurate conformer weights for measured observables, functional applications emphasize population differences above statistical uncertainty, and functional claims require identifying function-associated states and estimating their populations accurately.

Scientific question: Is the requested claim contract declared and internally consistent?

Runtime claim effect: A PASS only establishes a reviewable question; it emits no scientific verdict.

Implementation status: COMPLETE_DRAFT

Registry authority: PROPOSAL_ONLY_NOT_RUNTIME_ACTIVE

Family source grounding: PENDING_DOMAIN_REVIEW · Runtime readiness: NOT_READY

Narrow reusable use: PROJECT_INTERPRETATION: Require a declared CASE claim contract before source or edge review begins. The paper motivates intended-use-first evaluation but does not mandate CASE-first ordering or define the Atlas schema.

Forbidden generalization: Do not treat intended-use dependence as source validation, a prescribed Atlas CASE schema or predicate, an evidence-supported claim ceiling, or a terminal scientific conclusion.

Claim ceiling: Do not treat intended-use dependence as source validation, a prescribed Atlas CASE schema or predicate, an evidence-supported claim ceiling, or a terminal scientific conclusion.

NDSR-F01R02PENDING_DOMAIN_REVIEW

Family: F01_CLAIM_CONTRACT_AND_CEILING
Runtime subrule: F01R02_CASE_REQUESTED_WORDING_SCOPE

Locator: Sanabria et al. 2020, PDF pp.9-10, Discussion and concluding summary (local parsed derivative lines 785-835 and 915-930)

Locator status: REVIEW_DERIVATIVE_LOCATOR_REQUIRES_PRIMARY_PASSAGE_CHECK

Passage kind: UPSTREAM_REVIEW_DERIVATIVE_NOT_PRIMARY_VERBATIM

Source packet status: PENDING

Traceability note: No separate traceability note recorded.

Short passage: Primary-source observation: in the T4 lysozyme case, evidence for a third state, its population changes, microsecond exchange, and its proposed association with product release are distinct case-specific statements.

Atomic statement: For the studied T4 lysozyme variants, the authors separately report evidence for a third conformational state, relaxation times of about 4 and 230 microseconds, substrate- and mutation-associated population changes, and a proposed functional association with product release.

Scientific question: Is the requested wording inside the CASE intake's declared request scope?

Runtime claim effect: A PASS records intake scope only. It does not calculate an evidence-supported claim ceiling, which is deferred to Stage 2.

Implementation status: COMPLETE_DRAFT

Registry authority: PROPOSAL_ONLY_NOT_RUNTIME_ACTIVE

Family source grounding: PENDING_DOMAIN_REVIEW · Runtime readiness: NOT_READY

Narrow reusable use: PROJECT_INTERPRETATION: Record whether requested CASE wording concerns state existence, population, timescale, or functional association, while leaving the evidence-supported ceiling to a later evaluator. This reusable claim taxonomy is not stated as a universal scheme by the paper.

Forbidden generalization: Do not transfer the T4 lysozyme state, population, timescale, or proposed product-release association to another system, and do not infer kinetics, function, mechanism, or an evidence-supported ceiling from an intake scope check.

Claim ceiling: Do not transfer the T4 lysozyme state, population, timescale, or proposed product-release association to another system, and do not infer kinetics, function, mechanism, or an evidence-supported ceiling from an intake scope check.

Exact applicability, evidence, policy, and contract

Runtime subrule: F02R01_SOURCE_SAMPLE_SYSTEM_COMPOSITION_DECLARATION

Locator: Thomasen and Lindorff-Larsen 2022, Experimental methods p.543 (local parsed derivative lines 118-125 and 151-161); Future perspectives p.548 (lines 450-458)

Locator status: REVIEW_DERIVATIVE_LOCATOR_REQUIRES_PRIMARY_PASSAGE_CHECK

Passage kind: UPSTREAM_REVIEW_DERIVATIVE_NOT_PRIMARY_VERBATIM

Source packet status: LOCATOR_REVIEWED_FOR_PROPOSED_CONTROL_LOGIC

Traceability note: No separate traceability note recorded.

Short passage: Primary-source observation: integrative ensemble methods require observables that report on relevant structures and conditions, while the reviewed approaches assume isolated, chemically homogeneous pure-protein samples and require new methods for heterogeneous or polydisperse mixtures.

Atomic statement: The review describes current integrative approaches as refining ensembles of isolated protein species against measurements from chemically homogeneous pure-protein samples; heterogeneous or polydisperse mixtures require additional methods, and experimental perturbations remain part of interpretation.

Scientific question: Does this source declare the sample-system and composition context needed to avoid silently assuming a homogeneous single-species ensemble?

Runtime claim effect: A PASS records a source-local declaration only; it does not freeze a full construct schema or certify sample homogeneity.

Implementation status: COMPLETE_DRAFT

Registry authority: PROPOSAL_ONLY_NOT_RUNTIME_ACTIVE

Family source grounding: PENDING_DOMAIN_REVIEW · Runtime readiness: NOT_READY

Narrow reusable use: PROJECT_INTERPRETATION: Use these observations only for F02R01's SOURCE-local sample-system and composition declaration. PASS records declared context; it neither prescribes every construct field nor decides an EDGE relation.

Forbidden generalization: Do not infer a homogeneous chemical species from omitted metadata, treat missing metadata as proof of a mixture, or freeze a universal boundaries, mutations, or tags construct schema.

Claim ceiling: Do not infer a homogeneous chemical species from omitted metadata, treat missing metadata as proof of a mixture, or freeze a universal boundaries, mutations, or tags construct schema.

Atomic statement: The review states that data integrated as measurements of one conformational ensemble should use conditions such as buffer and temperature that are as similar as possible and should account for experiment-specific perturbations including fluorophore labelling, sample freezing, and alignment media.

Scientific question: Are the two source conditions recorded as compatible for the declared relation?

Runtime claim effect: A PASS records an explicit relation label only; it does not establish a universal comparability threshold.

Implementation status: COMPLETE_DRAFT

Family source grounding: PENDING_DOMAIN_REVIEW · Runtime readiness: NOT_READY

Narrow reusable use: PROJECT_INTERPRETATION: Use this observation only for F02R02's EDGE condition-relation classification: explicit compatibility may PASS, explicit mismatch may FAIL, and absent or ambiguous evidence remains UNRESOLVED.

Forbidden generalization: Do not infer comparability from a common modality or nominal label, invent a universal buffer or temperature tolerance, or conflate condition compatibility with validation independence.

Claim ceiling: Do not infer comparability from a common modality or nominal label, invent a universal buffer or temperature tolerance, or conflate condition compatibility with validation independence.

NDSR-F03R01PENDING_DOMAIN_REVIEW

Family: F03_SOURCE_MEASUREMENT_SEMANTICS
Runtime subrule: F03R01_SOURCE_NATIVE_MEASUREMENT

Locator: Fuertes et al. 2017, PDF pp.1-4 / PNAS E6342-E6345, Abstract, Introduction, and Results measurement passages (local full-text derivative lines 18-38, 95-136, 245-260, and 303-321)

Locator status: REVIEW_DERIVATIVE_LOCATOR_REQUIRES_PRIMARY_PASSAGE_CHECK

Passage kind: UPSTREAM_REVIEW_DERIVATIVE_NOT_PRIMARY_VERBATIM

Source packet status: LOCATOR_REVIEWED_FOR_PROPOSED_CONTROL_LOGIC

Traceability note: No separate traceability note recorded.

Short passage: Primary-source observation: SAXS-derived radius of gyration and smFRET-inferred dye distance have different spatial support and averaging, and smFRET distance inference depends on a model for the interdye-distance distribution.

Atomic statement: Fuertes et al. distinguish global SAXS radius of gyration from the smFRET-inferred dye-to-dye distance: the measurements interrogate different length scales and averaging, and the distance inference depends on an assumed interdye-distance distribution.

Scientific question: Does this source declare the native observable and measurement semantics needed to identify what it provides?

Runtime claim effect: A PASS records source-native measurement semantics only; it does not certify reliability, comparability, validation, or a scientific conclusion.

Implementation status: COMPLETE_DRAFT

Registry authority: PROPOSAL_ONLY_NOT_RUNTIME_ACTIVE

Family source grounding: PENDING_DOMAIN_REVIEW · Runtime readiness: NOT_READY

Narrow reusable use: PROJECT_INTERPRETATION: Use these observations only for F03R01's SOURCE-local native-measurement declaration. PASS records measurement semantics before relation-level evaluation; it does not decide comparability.

Forbidden generalization: Do not infer that different observables are automatically non-comparable, that all SAXS and smFRET pairs behave alike, or that a complete declaration establishes reliability, validation, a claim ceiling, or a final scientific verdict.

Claim ceiling: Do not infer that different observables are automatically non-comparable, that all SAXS and smFRET pairs behave alike, or that a complete declaration establishes reliability, validation, a claim ceiling, or a final scientific verdict.

NDSR-F06R01PENDING_DOMAIN_REVIEW

Family: F06_CROSS_SOURCE_COMPARABILITY_AND_EVIDENCE_ROLE
Runtime subrule: F06R01_SOURCE_EVIDENCE_ROLE

Locator: Bengtsen et al. 2020, PDF pp.7-8, Molecular dynamics simulations and Integrating experiments and simulations (local parsed derivative lines 292-312 and 344-394)

Locator status: REVIEW_DERIVATIVE_LOCATOR_REQUIRES_PRIMARY_PASSAGE_CHECK

Passage kind: UPSTREAM_REVIEW_DERIVATIVE_NOT_PRIMARY_VERBATIM

Source packet status: PENDING

Traceability note: No separate traceability note recorded.

Short passage: Primary-source observation: SAXS and NOE were used as refinement inputs, while PRE and EPR were later comparisons described as validation data not used directly in that refinement; the modalities constrain different structural properties.

Atomic statement: Bengtsen et al. used SAXS and NOE individually and jointly to refine the MD ensemble, then compared PRE and EPR data as validation because those data were not used directly as refinement inputs; the modalities report different structural information.

Scientific question: What exact evidence role and lineage does this source have?

Runtime claim effect: A PASS records a role; it does not establish validation independence.

Implementation status: COMPLETE_DRAFT

Registry authority: PROPOSAL_ONLY_NOT_RUNTIME_ACTIVE

Family source grounding: PENDING_DOMAIN_REVIEW · Runtime readiness: NOT_READY

Narrow reusable use: PROJECT_INTERPRETATION: Require a SOURCE role and lineage declaration before EDGE-level validation claims are considered. The formal SOURCE field and source-versus-edge split are repository methodology, not statements made by the paper.

Forbidden generalization: Do not call a source independent from its validation label alone, and do not transfer the paper's data-use roles to another case without a source-specific lineage record and EDGE audit.

Claim ceiling: Do not call a source independent from its validation label alone, and do not transfer the paper's data-use roles to another case without a source-specific lineage record and EDGE audit.

Family: F06_CROSS_SOURCE_COMPARABILITY_AND_EVIDENCE_ROLE
Runtime subrule: F06R02_EDGE_COMPARABILITY

Locator: Fuertes et al. 2017, PDF pp.7-9 / PNAS E6348-E6350, Discussion, Working Hypothesis, and Conclusion and Perspective (local full-text derivative lines 587-603, 622-629, and 749-761)

Locator status: REVIEW_DERIVATIVE_LOCATOR_REQUIRES_PRIMARY_PASSAGE_CHECK

Passage kind: UPSTREAM_REVIEW_DERIVATIVE_NOT_PRIMARY_VERBATIM

Source packet status: PENDING

Traceability note: No separate traceability note recorded.

Short passage: Primary-source observation: SAXS radius and smFRET distance can yield discrepant inferences because they probe different length scales and averaging; joint analysis can preserve complementary information.

Atomic statement: Fuertes et al. show that SAXS-derived radius of gyration and smFRET-inferred end-to-end distance can yield discrepant inferences because they interrogate different length scales and averaging, while joint use can constrain a mutually compatible ensemble.

Scientific question: Is the declared cross-source relation admissible after F02 condition and F03 measurement prerequisites resolve?

Runtime claim effect: A PASS requires recorded F02/F03 prerequisite PASS results. A FAIL blocks the declared comparison, not either source's local measurement.

Implementation status: COMPLETE_DRAFT

Registry authority: PROPOSAL_ONLY_NOT_RUNTIME_ACTIVE

Family source grounding: PENDING_DOMAIN_REVIEW · Runtime readiness: NOT_READY

Narrow reusable use: PROJECT_INTERPRETATION: Evaluate comparability at the EDGE while preserving source-local measurements. Explicit relation types, bridge fields, prerequisites, and NOT_COMPARABLE are repository control semantics, not paper-defined schema.

Forbidden generalization: Do not force numeric equivalence, erase disagreement by construction, infer that every SAXS and smFRET pair is non-comparable, or transfer this IDP-specific result universally.

Claim ceiling: Do not force numeric equivalence, erase disagreement by construction, infer that every SAXS and smFRET pair is non-comparable, or transfer this IDP-specific result universally.

NDSR-F06R03PENDING_DOMAIN_REVIEW

Family: F06_CROSS_SOURCE_COMPARABILITY_AND_EVIDENCE_ROLE
Runtime subrule: F06R03_EDGE_VALIDATION_INDEPENDENCE

Locator: Bengtsen et al. 2020, PDF p.8, PRE/EPR consistency check and Integrating experiments and simulations (local parsed derivative lines 344-348 and 369-394)

Locator status: REVIEW_DERIVATIVE_LOCATOR_REQUIRES_PRIMARY_PASSAGE_CHECK

Passage kind: UPSTREAM_REVIEW_DERIVATIVE_NOT_PRIMARY_VERBATIM

Source packet status: PENDING

Traceability note: No separate traceability note recorded.

Short passage: Primary-source observation: PRE and EPR were not direct BME refinement inputs, but the comparison reference NMR/EPR structure had itself been derived using PRE, EPR, and other NMR data.

Atomic statement: Bengtsen et al. describe PRE and EPR as validation data not used directly in the BME refinement, while also stating that the reference NMR/EPR structure was derived using PRE, EPR, and remaining NMR data.

Scientific question: Is a validation-independence label supported by declared lineage, data-sharing, and shared-error facts?

Runtime claim effect: A PASS records independence only. Comparability and F02/F03 prerequisites remain separate questions; a FAIL blocks only the independence label and retains the relation for human review.

Implementation status: COMPLETE_DRAFT

Registry authority: PROPOSAL_ONLY_NOT_RUNTIME_ACTIVE

Family source grounding: PENDING_DOMAIN_REVIEW · Runtime readiness: NOT_READY

Narrow reusable use: PROJECT_INTERPRETATION: Evaluate validation independence as an EDGE obligation from lineage, shared-data, and shared-error facts, separately from comparability. Not used directly in one refinement is not by itself a held-out or fully independent designation.

Forbidden generalization: Do not upgrade not-used-directly data, same-data evaluation, or shared-error agreement to held-out or independent validation without the declared lineage and shared-error audit.

Claim ceiling: Do not upgrade not-used-directly data, same-data evaluation, or shared-error agreement to held-out or independent validation without the declared lineage and shared-error audit.
